- **systematic-debugging**: Use when encountering any bug, test failure, or unexpected behavior — before proposing or writing a fix. Applies even when the user just says "it's broken" or pastes a stack trace without asking for a methodology. Registration (PROSPERO), search strategy, PICO, inclusion/exclusion, dual screening, risk of bias (ROB2/ROBINS-I), data extraction, GRADE certainty, PRISMA flow diagram, synthesis (narrative, meta-analysis, GRADE summary). Triggers on systematic review, scoping review, meta-analysis, PRISMA, PROSPERO, Cochrane review, umbrella review, realist synthesis. ([sulfierry/mcp](https://github.com/sulfierry/mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/systematic-review-prisma) · ★1)
- **systematic-review-prisma**: Runs a PRISMA 2020 systematic review: protocol, search strings, de-duplication, screening, extraction, and a checked flow diagram. Use for systematic or scoping reviews. Not for narrative reviews. ([Yash-Kavaiya/research-scholar-agent-skills](https://github.com/Yash-Kavaiya/research-scholar-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/systematic-review-prisma) · ★1)
- **systematic-review-prisma**: Plan, run and report systematic reviews and meta-analyses to PRISMA 2020 standards. Use when writing a review protocol (PROSPERO/OSF), building reproducible database search strings (PubMed, Embase, Scopus, Web of Science), deduplicating exports, organizing title/abstract and full-text screening, assessing risk of bias, drawing the PRISMA flow diagram, or completing the PRISMA 2020 checklist. Includes a dedup tool and a checked PRISMA diagram generator. ([Tashima-Tarsh/Disha6.6](https://github.com/Tashima-Tarsh/Disha6.6/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/kal-systematic-review-prisma))
- **systematic-review-prisma-workflow**: Use when a review must follow a reproducible systematic-review protocol, requiring an explicit search string and limits, duplicate removal, two-round screening with recorded exclusion reasons, per-study reporting appraisal, a PRISMA-style flow and a bounded synthesis with conflicts and gaps. ([CFZWzsc/bohr-p4-challenge-51977f9b](https://github.com/CFZWzsc/bohr-p4-challenge-51977f9b/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **systematic-review-protocol**: Specify the review before screening to reduce hindsight-driven decisions. Use when a researcher needs draft a transparent systematic-review protocol. ([voyager67/Reasoningai.studio](https://github.com/voyager67/Reasoningai.studio/tree/HEAD/skills/systematic-review-protocol) · ★3)
- **systematic-review-protocol**: Runs a systematic, scoping or rapid review to a standard somebody else could repeat and get the same set of papers. Produces a registered protocol written before searching, an answerable question in a structured frame, eligibility criteria with a reason attached to each, search strings recorded per database with platform and date and validated against a seed set of known papers, screening by two people with agreement reported, a flow of counts that reconciles, extraction to a piloted form, risk of bias assessed with a named tool that actually changes the synthesis, and a pooling decision made… ([ingridleiria/breaktalk-skills](https://github.com/ingridleiria/breaktalk-skills/tree/HEAD/phd-research/systematic-review-protocol))
- **systematic-review-protocol**: Create or amend a systematic-review protocol, review configuration, eligibility criteria, method-module selection, synthesis plan, and M1 approval subject. Use at the start of a new review or when a scientific protocol amendment is required. Do not execute searches or screen records. ([pinjun99/systematic-review-skills](https://github.com/pinjun99/systematic-review-skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/systematic-review-protocol))
- **systematic-review-protocol**: Write the protocol for a literature review before any search runs, covering the review type, a structured question frame, eligibility criteria, exact search strings per index, the search log format, the deduplication key order, staged screening with fixed exclusion reasons and the extraction table. Use when a body of academic or technical literature must be found, screened and synthesised reproducibly. Not for running the searches, screening the records or writing the review, and not for a single paper lookup. ([prez-tailered-ai/tailered](https://github.com/prez-tailered-ai/tailered/tree/HEAD/skills/apex-research/lanes/scholarly/systematic-review-protocol))
- **systematic-review-protocol-kit**: Required for every 系统性文献综述 case (/input/case.json): read this SKILL.md first and follow its steps with its bundled scripts; do not solve the case by hand. Systematic review on a supplied protocol and records: boolean search strings per database, two-round screening with clause ids and reasons, computed flow counts, quality appraisal tied to quotes, evidence-linked conclusions. ([jgwy/research-skills](https://github.com/jgwy/research-skills/tree/HEAD/research-03))
- **systematic-review-reporting-package**: Draft and reconcile a systematic-review manuscript, tables, figures, supplements, declarations, and publication package from approved evidence. Use a targeted claim ledger for consequential content and prepare the M5 subject after independent QC. Do not trace every ordinary scientific sentence or introduce new analyses silently. ([pinjun99/systematic-review-skills](https://github.com/pinjun99/systematic-review-skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/systematic-review-reporting-package))
- **systematic-review-risk-of-bias**: Perform design-appropriate risk-of-bias or critical appraisal when required by the approved protocol. Use current official tools, result-level targets where applicable, page-cited Tier 2 evidence, targeted independent checking, and human adjudication of material disagreements. Skip only when the active module and protocol make appraisal inapplicable. ([pinjun99/systematic-review-skills](https://github.com/pinjun99/systematic-review-skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/systematic-review-risk-of-bias))

- **systematic-review-screening**: Use when a task involves screening biomedical search results against a preregistered systematic-review protocol and inclusion criteria to identify the research or operational question, target population or system, relevant version, sensitive data, and approval boundary before acting. Use current primary sources, produce a traceable artifact, and verify it against explicit criteria. Trigger for planning, analysis, review, or troubleshooting in this focused domain; do not execute external writes or clinical actions without authorization. - **systematic-review-search-design**: Design reproducible systematic-review searches from an approved M1 protocol. - **systematic-review-search-execution**: Execute M2-approved search strategies and preserve reproducible raw results. Use for database, registry, website, citation, and supplementary searching. Do not change a strategy in production without returning the scientific change for approval. - **systematic-review-structured-synthesis**: Execute an M4-approved meta-analysis, other quantitative synthesis, structured synthesis without meta-analysis, or evidence map. Use the prespecified method, deterministic calculation checks, complete study accounting, and human resolution of consequential exceptions. Do not change methods in response to results. ([pinjun99/systematic-review-skills](https://github.com/pinjun99/systematic-review-skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/systematic-review-structured-synthesis))
- **systematic-review-synthesis-planning**: Confirm the prespecified synthesis plan against the included evidence, classify each synthesis group, document justified amendments, and prepare the M4 evidence-and-synthesis approval subject. Use after extraction and any applicable appraisal. Do not select methods because of observed significance or effect direction. ([pinjun99/systematic-review-skills](https://github.com/pinjun99/systematic-review-skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/systematic-review-synthesis-planning))
- **systematic-review-title-abstract-screening**: Screen titles and abstracts conservatively against the M1 eligibility contract. Do not retrieve full texts or require quotations for inclusions. - **systematic-root-cause-analysis**: - **Hypothesis 1 ($H_1$)**: *[Highest probability]* e.g., Missing null-check / unhandled `None` state when payload key is omitted. - **Hypothesis 2 ($H_2$)**: *[Moderate probability]* e.g., Race condition or out-of-order execution in asynchronous task queue. - **Hypothesis 3 ($H_3$)**: *[Lower probability / Edge case]* e.g., Type coercion mismatch during JSON serialization/deserialization. ([Shashank-VK/Build-it](https://github.com/Shashank-VK/Build-it/tree/HEAD/MASTER-VAULT/05-superior-os/.harness/skills/26-systematic-rca) · 3 copies)

- **systematic-study**: Use when designing or reviewing a study that varies parameters to produce an insight - an eval grid, a sweep, an ablation, a prompt-condition battery, a scaling ladder, an A/B of model variants, a panel comparing models by family, size or developer country.
